| Global Release Timer |
| --- |
| ![Image 383: Champions Meeting Taurus Cup Narrow](https://img.game8.co/4237767/75c0b7e93ca1424e169587c8a7282eae.png/show)**Champions Meeting: Taurus Cup** August 17, 2025 - August 27, 2025 |
| **Race Conditions**: Tokyo Turf 2,400m (Medium), Left ![Image 384: Spring](https://img.game8.co/4216026/b3066a3922f235046d758b2a749e8e94.png/show) Spring, ![Image 385: Spring](https://img.game8.co/4216251/1a4fc56486da476bf751c081a6f0973e.png/show) Sunny, Firm Ground * * * **Similar Race**: [Japanese Derby](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/539037) |

The **Champions Meeting: Taurus Cup** began on August 17, 2025 and lasted until August 27, 2025. This was the first Champions Meeting in the global version of Umamusume: Pretty Derby.

[Champions Meeting General Guide](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/541155)

### Taurus Cup Race Course Map

![Image 392: Taurus Cup Map](https://img.game8.co/4241619/91e0d8bd34b3c6a3d3596fbe06122f50.png/show)

* * *

**Race Conditions**: 

Tokyo Turf 2,400m (Medium), Left

![Image 393: Spring](https://img.game8.co/4216026/b3066a3922f235046d758b2a749e8e94.png/show) Spring, ![Image 394: Spring](https://img.game8.co/4216251/1a4fc56486da476bf751c081a6f0973e.png/show) Sunny, Firm Ground
Course Details
・Start of the late-race is at a corner (the final corner). 

┗ Distance from the start of the late-race to the remaining 777m is around 20m. 

・The final corner is around 50m after the start of the late-race. 

・There is a downhill at the middle of the race. 

・There are uphills in the early-race, mid-race, and late-race. 

┗ Recovery skills activated in the uphill are in the mid-race.

The Taurus Cup's race is a **Turf Medium** (2400m) racecourse that accommodates [Left-handed](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536967). Its distance is also a multiple of 400m, so it is [Standard Distance](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/537011). For a similar race you can emulate as the Taurus Cup, the closest is the [Japanese Derby (Tokyo Yushun)](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/539037).

### Aim for Building High Stamina

| ✕ | [![Image 404: Front Runner](https://img.game8.co/4252054/846150f2df4c6992658f72ff945cc05e.png/show)Front](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/544485) | [![Image 405: Pace Chaser](https://img.game8.co/4252055/18db0768c634f9eb9d96ec28c18a0ee2.png/show)Pace](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/544592) | [![Image 406: Late Surger](https://img.game8.co/4252056/eceba1efc9bb1af30ddf34275688b327.png/show)Late](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/544747) | [![Image 407: End Closer Icon](https://img.game8.co/4252057/7893b65717bffc236b0a88a9be789fd2.png/show)End](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/544879) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 Gold | A+ 900 | S+ 1000 | A+ 900 | A+ 900 |
| ![Image 408: Restless Icon](https://img.game8.co/4246887/be7c157b84ed26d82c150247dd5cf488.png/show)1 Gold | A 800 | A+ 900 | A 800 | A 800 |
| ![Image 409: Restless Icon](https://img.game8.co/4246887/be7c157b84ed26d82c150247dd5cf488.png/show)![Image 410: Restless Icon](https://img.game8.co/4246887/be7c157b84ed26d82c150247dd5cf488.png/show) 2 Gold | B+ 700 | A 800 | B+ 700 | B+ 700 |
| ⓘ **1000 Stamina** for any running style is optimal **if affordable**. Allows you to worry less about Debuffers. ⓘ **800 Stamina** is generally a good number even with **1-2 Gold Recovery** skills. ⓘ Risks can be made with 700 Stamina. But beware of Debuffers that can cut this to 600. ⓘ 600 Stamina and below is the danger zone for Medium races even with Gold Recovery Skills. |

※ Above are guidelines you can follow for building Stamina. This is based on our experience in public CM practices in Room Match.

※ Gold stands for a Gold Recovery Skill (ex. Gourmand).

For safe measure, we do not recommend going anywhere lower than 700 Stamina if you're up against serious contenders. 600 is a doable number but will risk you getting dragged far back by dedicated Debuffers like [Nice Nature](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536287) and [Grass Wonder](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536306).

#### Supplement Stamina with Recovery Skills

| Skill Rarity | Effect per Skill |
| --- | --- |
| ![Image 411: Recovery](https://img.game8.co/4217283/7d2eda4573d893b48de009055d140939.png/show) Gold Recovery Skill * * * ex. [Swinging Maestro](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536895) | Recovers approximately 180~200 Stamina. |
| ![Image 412: Recovery](https://img.game8.co/4217283/7d2eda4573d893b48de009055d140939.png/show) White Recovery Skill * * * ex. [Corner Recovery ◯](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/537006) | Recovers approximately 50~60 Stamina. |

The values above represent their effectiveness in a general sense. Different recovery skill effects and behavior will vary.

When aiming for maximum Speed and Power while having high Wit, it's expected that building high Stamina (especially more than 1000) in this scenario is a tall order.

This why it is recommended to **supplement Stamina with Recovery Skills** since they serve as efficient options to have if you cannot build enough stamina. Having them will also help **pad your stamina against debuff skills**.

The highest priority Speed skills are **speed skills that activate on the final corner** (or last spurt).

*   Highest priority: **Final Corner**, **Late-Race**, **Last Spurt** speed skills (ex. [Red Shift/LP1211-M](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536883)).
*   Next priority: **Mid-Race** skills that help you pick up speed and overtake potential blocks before the late-race.
*   Lower priority: **Early-Race** skills and strict conditionals that require being blocked. An exception to this would be the early-race skill [Groundwork](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/537044) for Front Runners, since, when combined with early-race skills and green passives, helps catapult you to the front, avoiding immediate blocks.

#### Maruzensky's Red Shift is the Best Speed Inherit for Front and Pace

[Maruzensky](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536319)'s [Red Shift](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536883) is so far the best unique skill that you can pass down to Front Runners and Pace Chasers. Though, it may also be passed down to Late Surgers or End Closers if you don't have the other Late/End uniques.

This skill is also often paired with [Taiki Shuttle](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536317)'s [Shooting for Victory](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536881)! which increases your acceleration when you are well-positioned at the Final Corner (near the same time with Red Shift).

The Tier List above were the projected ranks for the Champions Meeting Taurus Cup. The Taurus Cup is a 2400m Medium race with similar characteristics to the [Japanese Derby](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/539038). We went with the criteria below for ranking them:

*   **Unqiue skill strength**. [Maruzensky](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536319) has one of the best late-race acceleration skills so far in global. Another notable Uma is [Taiki Shuttle](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536317), however, she ranks mostly for her unique inherit [Shooting for Victory!](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536881) since she has an E aptitude for Medium by default. 
*   **Appropriate aptitude** for the race (Medium). This covers even those whose Medium aptitudes are a bit off but can be covered by the necessary sparks.
*   **Debuff capability**. [Nice Nature](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536287) and other Umas with Dominator, such as [Symboli Rudolf](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536314), [Agnes Tachyon](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536293), and [Grass Wonder](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536306) can find themselves higher or lower on the list depending on the strength of their debuffs.

[Best Character (Trainee) Tier List](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536352)

### Highly Recommended Umamusume

| Notable Racers | Details |
| --- | --- |
| [![Image 1214: Oguri Cap (Starlight Beat) Icon](https://img.game8.co/4215120/b88f532cf2ad5d92e2cba38d4e3e1967.png/show)Oguri Cap (Starlight Beat)](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536318) | ・Easy to train due to aptitude in Mile and Medium. ・Has a strong last spurt acceleration skill by default ([Triumphant Pulse](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536882)). ・Becomes significantly stronger when inheriting other late-race acceleration skills like [Red Shift/LP1211-M](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536883) and [Shooting for Victory!](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536881) ・Best suited as a **Pace Chaser**. |
| [![Image 1215: Maruzensky (Formula R) Icon](https://img.game8.co/4215129/d710f507283c55a35b5a5ca256380ef3.png/show)Maruzensky (Formula R)](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536319) | ・Is the best parent Uma for passing down [Red Shift/LP1211-M](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536883) to Front Runner/Pace Chaser trainees. ・Noticeably easy career like Oguri Cap, proficient already in Medium and Mile. ・Must built as a strong **Front Runner** to be effective (skills that bring you quickly to the lead like [Groundwork](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/537044) will help greatly). |
| [![Image 1216: Gold Ship (Red Strife) Icon](https://img.game8.co/4215126/f865ae8805e1a047c197b41dcf629128.png/show)Gold Ship (Red Strife)](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536305) | ・Oneo f the strongest **End Closers**, able to carry the race in case the Front Runner or Pace Chaser of the race falls back. ・Best built at **Potential Lvl. 5** where the recovery skill [Go-Home Specialist](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536925) becomes available. ・Best to boost late-race acceleration further with [Behold Thine Emperor's Divine Might](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536889) and [Let's Pump Some Iron!](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536872) (Feel the Burn!). |
| [![Image 1217: Nice Nature (Poinsettia Ribbon) Icon](https://img.game8.co/4215099/f427b728c8fff6a14b9db7b1c88edab0.png/show)Nice Nature (Poinsettia Ribbon)](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536287) | One the most efficient **Debuffers** for Medium. Has several debuffs as part of its Potential Level unlocks, notably [Mystifying Murmur](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536891) and [All-Seeing Eyes](https://game8.co/games/Umamusume-Pretty-Derby/archives/536906). ・Does not require to have very high stats to compete. You only need to make sure she has high **Wit** to proc her skills (above 500 is recommended) and high enough **Stamina** to catch up with the pack in front of her as a Late Surger. |
